# DataHub locked analysis

The frozen analyzer was verified byte for byte against `c15abdcb7b990c94223670d45119eb59b6218b7e`. This report renders its [analysis JSON](datahub_analysis_1cf9dce0.json) from the [locked record](datahub_final_1cf9dce0.json) tagged `egpot-datahub-locked-v1`.

## A. Confirmatory contrasts C1–C4

Point estimates and intervals are in percentage points of the pooled seed-0 full-hit rate. The paired start-node bootstrap used 10,000 draws with `RandomState(0)`, 26 clusters and 52 queries. The four percentile intervals are 98.75% Bonferroni-adjusted, with 0.00625 in each tail. For C1 and C2 the strongest baseline was re-selected inside each resample. The table names the strongest baseline in the observed data.

| Contrast | Budget | First method | Observed comparator | Difference (pp) | 98.75% interval (pp) | Verdict |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| C1 | 10 | `egpot_full_w4` | `scalar_beam` | +38.46 | [+19.23, +55.77] | supported |
| C2 | 40 | `egpot_full_w12` | `mcts_standard` | +32.69 | [+11.54, +51.92] | supported |
| C3 | 10 | `egpot_full_w4` | `egpot_full_w12` | +53.85 | [+38.46, +69.23] | supported |
| C4 | 40 | `egpot_full_w4` | `egpot_no_contracts_w4` | +36.54 | [+19.23, +55.77] | supported |

## B. Predictions — exploratory

| Preregistered wording | Verdict | Observed evidence |
| --- | --- | --- |
| “PPR will lose ground because 40 nodes cover a tiny share of this graph.” | Held directionally | At budget 40 PPR had 22/52 (42.3%), versus 48/52 (92.3%) for EG-POT width 12. The comparison does not establish that graph coverage caused the gap. |
| “Width-4 EG-POT will retain its tight-budget advantage.” | Held | At budget 10 width 4 had 41/52 (78.8%), versus 21/52 (40.4%) for scalar beam, the strongest observed baseline (C1). |
| “Width 12 will cost hits at budget 10.” | Held | Width 12 had 13/52 (25.0%), versus 41/52 (78.8%) at width 4 (C3). |
| “Contract consistency will matter at budget 40.” | Held | Width 4 had 42/52 (80.8%) with contract consistency, versus 23/52 (44.2%) in the ablation (C4). |

No prediction failed in its stated direction. The proposed explanation for the PPR gap was not tested.

## C. Full hits at seed 0 — exploratory

All 26 links were covered. Builder-coverage exclusions: 0. Each cell gives full hits over queries and the rate. A full hit requires both exact gold IDs. A dash means the protocol did not schedule that budget.

## E. Descriptive measures and paired compactness — exploratory

The metrics tables use seed 0. Nodes to hit and precision at hit are hits-only means. A dash marks an undefined hits-only mean. Latency is the recorded mean per query in seconds. Paired compactness compares the same queries both methods hit, and the table gives that subset size.

## H. Handler-class sensitivity check — exploratory

This paired bootstrap used 10,000 draws and a fresh `RandomState(0)`. Clusters are source-qualified Java handler classes. The same tie-breaking and per-resample baseline re-selection apply. These unadjusted intervals do not determine the confirmatory verdicts.
